- **niw-rfe-response**: Works a USCIS Request for Evidence or Notice of Intent to Deny on an EB-2 NIW petition, from reading the notice through to a drafted response. Enforces the rules that decide these responses: eligibility is fixed as of the filing date, so facts that arose after filing cannot establish it, and the proposed endeavor may not be re-scoped to fit the notice even when the notice asks for a more detailed description… ([thepaperscompany/niw-skills](https://github.com/thepaperscompany/niw-skills/tree/HEAD/thepapers-niw/skills/niw-rfe-response))

Use when modules mix data with behavior, options are loose, service wiring is duplicated, or host/user boundaries are unclear. ([caniko/ai-skills](https://github.com/caniko/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/global_skills/nix-module-design))
- **nix-module-dev**: Nix language, module system, overlay mechanics, and derivation authoring at production depth. Use when creating custom modules, overlays, derivations, or modifying flake inputs. ([kressnerd/nix-config](https://github.com/kressnerd/nix-config/tree/HEAD/.maki/skills/nix-module-dev) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **nix-module-dev**: **Applies to**: architect, code, debug **Trigger**: Creating custom modules, overlays, derivations, or modifying flake inputs ([kressnerd/nix-config](https://github.com/kressnerd/nix-config/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/nix-module-dev) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **nix-module-introspection**: Use when inspecting Nix module options from flake inputs. ([darkmatter/hermes](https://github.com/darkmatter/hermes/tree/HEAD/skills/devops/nix-module-introspection))
